## Overview & Instructions

This catalog lists tracks that exist in your manual backups (`Music.zip`, `Music Feb 2025`, and `Music Sync`) but are **not** present in your active listening library on `E:\Music`.

> [!TIP] How to Use This Catalog
> 1. Review the table below or open the CSV file in Excel/Sheets.
> 2. Many of these tracks may be intentional prunes. However, if you spot tracks that accidentally disappeared, simply mark them for restoration.
> 3. Once reviewed, we can batch-restore the desired tracks directly into `E:\Music` before consolidating or deleting any backups.
